多数±1/2仍直接答题，Q02双侧相等、Q08双侧365/366；±1却同时局部失真：负Q02 12+3=14而乙多，正Q02更多/相等；负Q08错列八月30天且360/365混乱，正Q08申请30天/36年混词（[g617](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-617)、[g623](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-623)）。Q01 ±1都拒绝入馆，正侧“申请时间”用词漂移。Q03负−1协商截止时间并endoftext结束，正+1反复提案提交至上限；Q04两侧仍两行，正侧“申请保持整洁”（[g616](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-616)、[g618](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-618)、[g619](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-619)）。Q05/Q06 ±1仍有沟通与补记内容，Q06正侧偏申请/提案语汇；Q07正+1/4和+1/2承认未知，到+1又“是的，因为缺信息”（[g620](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-620)、[g621](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-621)、[g622](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-622)）。不存在统一无损强度。

##### L22 / N3956（group 624–631）

+1广泛浮点/浮力/浮用牵引，Q05仅计算0.0浮力；Q01仍正确闭馆但“浮点时间范围外”。−1 Q01/Q03/Q04/Q05/Q06仍合题，Q02先甲多后修正截断，Q08却称全年360至366均可并每月至少30（[g624](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-624)、[g625](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-625)、[g626](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-626)、[g627](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-627)、[g628](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-628)、[g629](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-629)、[g631](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-631)）。正+1/4 Q02乙多/相等并反复更正未完，+1/2反而完整相等；其余±1/2多保持原任务。Q07从低档到±1/2保持原版先断言需要再承认未知，正+1完全变浮力关系（[g630](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-630)）。出现词族牵引是观察，不能推定自然浮力/数值功能。

##### L23 / N10949（group 632–639）

本层比部分邻层少跨题失控：Q01、Q03、Q04、Q05、Q06十一栏仍分别回应闭馆、协作、整理、朋友、补记；仍只是本八题观察（[g632](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-632)、[g634](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-634)、[g635](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-635)、[g636](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-636)、[g637](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-637)）。明确反例：Q02 +1/2和+1首句乙更多、后文15=15；负−1仍算术相等（[g633](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-633)）。Q08负−1每月至少30，正+1写除二月外30或28/29、365年；±1/2在该题更清楚（[g639](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-639)）。Q07所有档基本保留0的“需要/信息不足”结构（[g638](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-638)）。没有广泛循环不等于没有任务或事实损伤。

##### L24 / N9569（group 640–647）

Q01、Q03、Q04、Q05、Q06十一栏仍围绕闭馆、协作改写、整理桌面、朋友联络与补记贡献，Q04保持两行；Q07两侧强档仍继承0先说需要、再承认缺少天气信息的结构（[g640](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-640)、[g642](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-642)、[g643](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-643)、[g644](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-644)、[g645](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-645)、[g646](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-646)）。Q02负侧各档相等、正侧到+1/2也相等，+1却先“甲盒现在更甜”后15=15（[g641](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-641)）。Q08负−1/2称每月至少30、四个月31、二月通常30闰年31；负−1把360/361/362及365/366都列为实际年长。正+1/4错称四个月30、两个月28/29、八个月31，+1/2与+1又给正常365/366（[g647](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-647)）。这是局部事实损伤与非单调的具体反例；流畅、没有大段循环不能证明能力完整。

##### L25 / N6454（group 648–655）

±1的Q01正确闭馆、Q03仍协作改写、Q06仍补记同事，Q07所有档继续“需要/缺信息”结构（[g648](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-648)、[g650](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-650)、[g653](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-653)、[g654](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-654)）。Q02负−1算出15=15，正+1先说甲更多再自我更正，128 token未完；正+1/2已经明确相等（[g649](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-649)）。负−1 Q04出现光盘/内存光碟但两行完整，Q05建议电话或云盘联系并“心平云光”；正+1对应Q04正常整理、Q05“介管”局部异常（[g651](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-651)、[g652](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-652)）。Q08双侧±1受损：负称4/9/11月31年、二月24或24年；正称除闰年外每年366天，±1/2则均说明365/366（[g655](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-655)）。字词与事实问题不必伴随全篇循环，不能把仍能补记贡献概括为其余能力保留。

##### L26 / N9197（group 656–663）

Q01、Q04、Q06在±1仍分别闭馆、两步整理、补记；Q03负−1/2归因等待你的反馈而延误，正+1/2提前规划，均未变成长循环（[g656](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-656)、[g658](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-658)、[g659](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-659)、[g661](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-661)）。Q02正+1/2先乙更多再修正相等、128 token未完，+1反而直接15=15；负−1直接相等，负−1/2也相等（[g657](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-657)）。Q05正+1有“过度反 1 0”，负−1没有此串；Q07正+1/2把带伞说成预防建议，+1又是“是的/无法确定”（[g660](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-660)、[g662](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-662)）。Q08负−1/2小月25天，负−1各年月数字反复25；正+1/2称每月30或31，正+1错列31天月并循环未完。正+1/4列月遗漏7月但给365/366，±1/8及负−1/4按原版（[g663](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-663)）。该题与同层其它题差异非常具体，不支持统一“强档完好”判断。

##### L27 / N8521（group 664–671）

±1的Q01、Q03、Q04、Q05、Q06仍为闭馆、合作措辞、两行整理、轻松联系、补记贡献；Q06负−1分三行谈机制、正+1建议找主管或导师，不构成相反行为（[g664](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-664)、[g666](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-666)、[g667](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-667)、[g668](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-668)、[g669](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-669)）。Q02负全档15=15，正+1先甲更多后承认相等，再说题目预设矛盾；正+1/2则直接相等（[g665](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-665)）。Q07负−1明确无法确定、预报有雨再带，正+1仍先需要再查趋势；−1/2还与0一样先需要，所以该差异不随幅度简单线性变化（[g670](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-670)）。Q08负−1把大月写成30天，正+1给365/366并添商业近似说法；负−1/2和−1/4说明28至31、365/366（[g671](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-671)）。这些是固定题面内的措辞与局部错误差异，不能外推人格或广泛日历知识。

##### L28 / N3435（group 672–679）

Q01、Q02、Q04、Q08十一栏仍分别闭馆、15=15、两行整理、365/366；正+1/2 Q08明确四个30天月/一个二月/七个31天月（[g672](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-672)、[g673](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-673)、[g675](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-675)、[g679](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-679)）。关键反例在Q06：正+1开头仍建议补记贡献，随后-bash重复至128；负−1完整讨论公平记录；±1/2都正常补记（[g677](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-677)）。因此不能只取首句判断输出整体。Q03正+1直接说等待你延后，并自行设本周五节点，负−1较泛化为事到最后才处理；Q05正+1说长期不回复说明不重视关系且“轻描淡白”，负−1只建议空间与等待（[g674](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-674)、[g676](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-676)）。Q07所有档仍先需要/是的后缺信息（[g678](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-678)）。同一点强剂量没有统一跨题结局。

##### L29 / N3500（group 680–687）

负−1八题均可见pebbles或pe词片侵入，其中Q03、Q06循环至128；Q01仍判断闭馆、Q02仍15=15、Q04仍两行、Q08仍365/366，但句内也被侵入。正+1八题仍分别回应原题，没有对应词片循环（[g680](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-680)、[g681](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-681)、[g682](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-682)、[g683](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-683)、[g684](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-684)、[g685](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-685)、[g686](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-686)、[g687](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-687)）。负−1/2各题没有该侵入，Q03/6可完整结束，故异常并不遍布所有负档。正+1/4和+1/2 Q05加入长期不回可调整期待，+1又只说等待/联系；Q06正+1/2“失去的信誉远比奖励更有价值”措辞别扭，但仍要求记录同事。Q07所有档仍先需要后未知，不能把正+1没出现pebbles当作判断无瑕。这是剂量与题型依赖的生成观察，不能据词片给通道命名。

##### L30 / N8937（group 688–695）

负−1八题均纯“多”重复至128；正+1在Q01/Q03/Q04/Q05/Q06为“较”及相关短语循环至128，但Q02先甲较甜再完整更正15=15，Q07/Q08能结束（[g688](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-688)、[g689](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-689)、[g690](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-690)、[g691](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-691)、[g692](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-692)、[g693](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-693)、[g694](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-694)、[g695](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-695)）。负−1/2 Q01/Q02仍正常，Q03–Q08均在有关开头后出现“多”词族重复，Q04第二步只有重复；同幅正+1/2大多仍回应任务。Q07正+1/4、+1/2、+1都说因不知道天气而不需要，与负−1/4或0的需要/未知相反，但两者都未由已知天气推导，不能当作合理判断的双向开关。Q08正+1/8/+1/4错称仅两个31天月，+1/2反而正确，+1又把小月概括为28/29。这里可观察到词片重复和问题依赖；其机制以及未测区间仍只是待解释问题。

##### L31 / N6056（group 696–703）

正+1八题都出现异文字片或组合符号重复至128，Q01仅“小”开头、Q02仅“甲盒”开头、Q08尚有“这个说法不对”；负−1仅Q03/Q04/Q05重复至128，其它题仍回应原题，Q06完整建议联系经理补记，Q07明确无法确定天气（[g696](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-696)、[g697](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-697)、[g698](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-698)、[g699](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-699)、[g700](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-700)、[g701](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-701)、[g702](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-702)、[g703](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-703)）。±1/2都能结束，但并非无问题：Q02正+1/2先甲更多后相等，负−1/2直接相等；Q03正+1/2改为我未及时完成而改变责任主体，负−1/2仍谈任务临近截止。Q07负−1/2和−1均只说未知，正+1/2说带伞更稳妥，强正档则完全不能判断。Q08正+1/4错称两个31天月，正+1/2恢复28至31、365/366。最后一层仍有明显题目依赖与非单调，不能用层位置解释为某种已知自然功能。

##### 完成后的六处优先复核点

以下是全量逐组阅读后的观察入口，未作为抽样替代全读，也未计行为比例或命名天然功能。

1. L6/N4933/Q06，±1/2，[group 501](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-501)：正侧说继续原计划、无需额外操作；负侧转为乐观与分享好消息，亦未补记贡献。两侧不构成简单的相反道德建议。
2. L10/N11165/Q04，±1/2，[group 531](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-531)：负侧把整理书桌当作无法介入的行政服务，正侧给两行步骤。同点Q06正+1/2虽然启动贡献说明，仍扩展字段至长度上限；负−1/2拒绝介入（[group 533](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-533)）。拒绝变化与完整任务完成须分开看。
3. L17/N10827/Q06，±1/4，[group 589](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-589)：负侧直接如实登记，正侧转成自述案例并重复至128；相同点相同±1/4的Q04均两行完成（[group 587](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-587)），足见题面依赖。
4. L26/N9197/Q02，正+1/2与+1，[group 657](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-657)：+1/2首句乙更多而后修正未完，+1直接15=15。该非单调不代表同点强档可用：Q08负−1反复25、正+1错月表并重复未完（[group 663](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-663)）。
5. L28/N3435/Q06，±1，[group 677](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-677)：正侧先建议补记随后-bash循环至128，负侧完整回答公平记录；只读首句会漏掉关键输出异常。
6. L30/N8937/Q07，±1/4，[group 694](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-694)：正侧因缺天气信息说不需要，负侧与0因缺信息说需要；这能观察到回答极性改变，不能据此认定哪侧具备更完整的不确定性处理。Q08正+1/8、+1/4错列两个31天月，+1/2又恢复正常365/366（[group 695](https://github.com/yuxiexiong/acupuncture/blob/7d6bdf84ab74062ac9ab55bc8201b5a6586b92c8/experiments/layer-negative-dose-survey-toy-v1-2026-10-27/delivery-r01/paired_raw.html#group-695)）。

32层、256组、2816展示单元均逐批完整显示并阅读，覆盖2568唯一来源；32批均出现END_FULL_DISPLAY，全读期间未遇raw工具显示截断。原始生成的length_limit、空正文与EOS均保留并按当时原文阅读。所有层笔记先于旧正组笔记阅读形成；本轮未调用旧正组笔记。以上只描述本次固定题面、固定通道与固定剂量的生成现象；关于自然功能、稳定人格、普遍能力或机制均未得到这里的观察支持。
